  MSI: C:\Projects\hazina\apps\Demos\Hazina.Demo.AgenticOrchestration.Installer\bin\Release\HazinaOrchestrationSetup.msi

  What the MSI does:
    - Kills any running HazinaOrchestration.exe
    - Removes old Windows Service (if exists, migration from v1)
    - Installs to: C:\Program Files\Hazina Orchestration\
    - Creates Start Menu shortcut
    - Includes React SPA + 6 asset files
    - Shows license agreement during install
    - Finish dialog offers to run Setup.ps1 (Tailscale/HTTPS config)

  After install:
    Finish dialog checkbox launches Setup.ps1 for Tailscale/HTTPS setup
    Or launch from Start Menu / run HazinaOrchestration.exe
    System tray icon with context menu (dashboard, swagger, exit)
    Toggle 'Start with Windows' from tray menu
    Web UI:  https://localhost:5123
    Swagger: https://localhost:5123/swagger
